# Skills · B · part 67

827 entries, boltz-small-molecule-adme to book-description. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **boltz-small-molecule-adme**: Predict Tier-1 ADME/ADMET for small molecules with Boltz from bare SMILES — no target, no docking. Use when the user wants solubility, permeability, or lipophilicity/logD for a molecule or list of molecules. Not for ranking molecules against a protein target (use boltz-small-molecule-screen, which already returns ADME free). ([openai/plugins](https://github.com/openai/plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/boltz-api-cli/skills/boltz-small-molecule-adme) · ★8k · 31 copies)
- **boltz-small-molecule-design**: Design new small-molecule binders with Boltz. Use when generating novel ligands or hits for a target without a fixed compound library. Not for screening existing molecules or one-off docking. ([openai/plugins](https://github.com/openai/plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/boltz-api-cli/skills/boltz-small-molecule-design) · ★8k · 31 copies)
- **boltz-small-molecule-screen**: Screen existing small-molecule libraries with Boltz. Use when docking, scoring, or ranking a supplied SMILES or compound library against a target; also returns free Tier-1 ADME/ADMET (solubility, permeability, lipophilicity/logD) per molecule. Not for de novo molecule design, one-off docking, or ADME on bare SMILES with no target (use boltz-small-molecule-adme). ([openai/plugins](https://github.com/openai/plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/boltz-api-cli/skills/boltz-small-molecule-screen) · ★8k · 30 copies)

- **boltzconf**: How confident is Boltz about THE BINDER and its interface, rather than the whole complex? Writes binder pLDDT, `chains_ptm`, symmetrised `pair_chains_iptm`, contact-restricted interface PAE and ipSAE, with trust columns and comparability keys. Load before composing a boltzconf run or interpreting its numbers. Traps: the chain-index→letter map of the JSON is unchecked in-tool, `ipae_*` is not a BindCraft `i_pae`, and an all-NA ipSAE is a result, not a fault. ([jlmoraleshellin/ant-comp](https://github.com/jlmoraleshellin/ant-comp/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/boltzconf) · ★1)

- **bonferroni-corrected-t-test-comparison**: Use when comparing per-step removal (or depletion) rates of a spiked/known-positive class against the background class across replicates, and multiple filter steps require multiplicity correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bonferroni-corrected-t-test-comparison))
- **bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a strand asymmetry analysis produces many P-values from repeated two-tailed binomial tests (p = 0.5 expected asymmetry) and you need Bonferroni-corrected significance for each feature (TF PWM, motif, repeat class, ChIP-seq peak set). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction--c5141cf3432496d0))
- **bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when many p-values are produced simultaneously (e.g., 48 K-S tests per lane pair on genome-partitioned read distributions) and per-combination significance calls must be corrected for multiple testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction--71dc0263531bea9f))
- **bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when many correlated biomarkers (e.g., 226 Nightingale NMR metabolomics measures) are simultaneously tested and an explicit family-wise alpha split (0.05 divided by the number of tests) is needed to declare significance or prune a model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction--e1ea591b960ee42e))
- **bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when performing self-contained globaltest feature-set testing over spectral-similarity-derived k-medoids clusters in LC-MS/MS untargeted metabolomics data, and cluster-wise corrected P-values across multiple contrasts are required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction--2eae0bc460667d0c))
- **bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a benchmarking workflow produces one P value per metabolite per feature set (e.g. Pearson correlations from 2,025 cross-validated RF models) and a per-metabolite significance call is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction--e42b45a79037db2d))
- **bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when when you have performed many pairwise correlation tests between candidate parent and adduct ion intensity pairs in MSI data and need to identify statistically significant relationships while controlling for multiple-comparison bias. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction))
- **bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a univariate linear model is fitted on each column of an NMR peak-value feature matrix (e.g., 31 x 677 from speaq 2.0) and raw p-values must be family-wise corrected before declaring significant features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction--106c56395d33cfcc))
- **bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when comparing abundances of many predicted functional modules (e.g. KEGG modules M00009, M00240) between sample groups and need family-wise error control over the resulting battery of statistical tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction))
- **bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a large family of statistical tests (e.g., 1641 motif enrichment Wilcoxon tests over a gene set) must be jointly thresholded, and raw p-values would otherwise yield inflated false positives. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction--ad8b484023928d59))
- **bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a per-gene model (e.g., the negative binomial GLM with Time, Group, and Time-by-Group interaction) yields thousands of raw p-values that must be Bonferroni-adjusted before calling tDEGs at p.cutoff = 0.05. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction--b9ad0fbc27d7b21c))
- **bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when many module-level or gene-set-level statistics (e.g., dozens of leiden-derived GRN module scores) are tested against the same phenotype and individual uncorrected p-values would inflate the false positive rate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction--c505bd39023d31d2))
- **bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when testing many source–sink causal paths per subnetwork (SEMace effect='source2sink') or declaring perturbed nodes/edges in SEMtree modules, and family-wise error control at alpha=0.05 is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction--adc88ef57ae9d929))
- **bonferroni-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when thousands of genes are tested in parallel by CTMM (e.g., REML JK and HE Wald p-values per gene) and a strict family-wise-error-controlled gene hit list is required before ranking or cross-method intersection. - **book-ddia-data-design**: Guides data-intensive design checks for consistency, reliability, schema evolution, and data flow risks. In strict tasks, mandatory before design stabilizes or implementation begins when changing persisted/shared data, schemas, migrations, shared/persistent/cross-request/cross-process caches, async or cross-service flows, data ownership, or recovery; in default/lite tasks, use on demand when those risks or explicit delivery requirements are present. - **book-deconstruction**: 对书籍进行章节级要点提炼与深度解读，生成大纲级别结构化笔记和配套思维导图。当用户要求"拆解一本书"、"分析某本书"、"提取书的核心要点"、"生成读书笔记"、"做思维导图"、"拆书"、"book analysis"或类似意图时触发。支持PDF上传、书名搜索、URL链接、电子书文件等多种输入来源。 ([x2sin1x/myskills](https://github.com/x2sin1x/myskills/tree/HEAD/skills/book-deconstruction))
- **book-deconstructor**: 知识拆解·行动化框架。将非虚构类书籍转化为结构化、可执行的笔记手册。 Not a summary — an actionable operations manual you could take into the field. Pipeline: 文本提取(EPUB→TXT) → 全文深读 → 知识单元识别 → 模板化结构 → 迭代精炼 → 输出(MD+PDF)。 内置书籍类型路由(技术操作/概念思辨/医学/历史叙事)，自动适配模板结构。 每一知识单元按「原理→材料→步骤→误区→检验」五段模板组织。 支持 v1(摘要)→v2(扩写 8-10x)→v3(精校) 三遍迭代。 触发词：「拆书」「结构化笔记」「做笔记」「book notes」「知识解构」。EN triggers: deconstruct book, book analysis, analyze book, book deep dive, book argument map, book breakdown, reverse engineer a book. 「actionable notes」「操作手册笔记」「知识结构化」「把这本书拆成笔记」 「非虚构笔记」「book deconstruct」「书籍结构化」 「原理」「所需材料和工具」「常见误区与修正」「成品检验标准」「详细操作步骤」。 不在普通闲聊、纯文学阅读、虚构小说赏… ([KIRRAWA/cogniforge](https://github.com/KIRRAWA/cogniforge/tree/HEAD/skills/book-deconstructor) · ★1)
- **book-deep-dive**: 当用户要精读一本书、整理本地电子书或微信读书划线、制作读书总结/知识脉络图/一页纸/学习卡片箱知识卡片，或增量更新读书笔记时使用。也适用于尚未开读、没有划线的原文领读，以及修正已有读书卡片。多书筛选或把书做成可调用技能包不属于本技能。 ([zhengmeiyang5-boop/book-deep-dive](https://github.com/zhengmeiyang5-boop/book-deep-dive/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **book-deep-read**: 为用户生成一份关于某本书的深度阅读报告，带结构标题，适合深度阅读和知识内化，报告结尾附有互动问答环节。 当用户说"给我《XX》的阅读报告"、"帮我解读《XX》"、"我想深入了解《XX》"、"我读完了《XX》，帮我做报告"、 "《XX》读书笔记"、"书评《XX》"、"帮我快速了解《XX》"、"我想把《XX》和《YY》比较"时，必须触发本 Skill。 支持七种报告类型：入门精读型、读后深化型、重读唤醒型、精华闪读型、应用实践型、批判辩论型、对比阅读型。 报告目的是帮助普通读者加深记忆、与既有知识产生联系、产生洞见，并替代逐字阅读快速掌握核心。 报告结尾必须启动一对一问答互动，每次只出一题，等用户回答后给出点评再出下一题。 ([Nickilism/Skills](https://github.com/Nickilism/Skills/tree/HEAD/book-deep-read) · ★1)
